- 2026-07-14 · v0.46 · **Knowledge-retrieval layer (RAG) — §18.5 (author question: "What is RAG — do we already have that?" Finding: the governance half existed, the retrieval architecture is now added — at Universe level).** **New Golden Rules (§2):** `[STANDARD]` **Index as a derived artifact with freshness coupling** (source version stamp per chunk; source release → reindex; stale chunks flagged/excluded — eighth instance of "nothing ages unobserved": the AI never answers unnoticed from stale knowledge); `[HARD]` **rights-filtered retrieval** (chunks inherit the data class + rights of the source; filtering at query time at the retrieval endpoint — RAG is never a rights bypass; "visibility is not security" applied to knowledge); `[STANDARD]` **citation obligation** (every answer references the artifact + version of its source chunks; source-free portions visibly marked "freely stated"); `[STANDARD]` **search degradation** (AI off/budget/provider outage → classic search with hits instead of a formulated answer — loss of convenience, never of control) with tier mapping (**retrieval/embeddings = I1**, token-free, model governance · **generation = I2**, locked-down RAG prompts, budgeted · distillation bridge for frequent stable questions). **§18.5:** process, building blocks, first candidate use case (the master itself as a queryable knowledge base for the developer team); chunking/embedding method `[OPEN]`. **§6:** RAG-retrieval sentinel [HARD]. ToDo extended by three v0.46 items. Source: exclusively this chat + the existing v0.45.
- 2026-07-14 · v0.45 · **Split panes: dividable work surfaces with self-explanatory dividers (author's find via screenshot "Click to collapse ⌘B · Drag to resize"; finding: this was missing).** New Golden Rule (§2 `[STANDARD]`): wherever views have adjacent panes, dividers are **draggable** (resize) and **clickable** (expand/collapse) — **vertical, horizontal, nested within each other**, each level independent; every divider **explains itself on hover** (tooltip + shortcut, contrast ≥ 4.5:1); collapsing is visibly indicated and reversible (hide-not-delete in the layout); the split is remembered per user/view (pattern: saved views); keyboard- and touch-operable (Aria); usage explained per view template ("where wanted and useful" — adjacent panes without a pane decision = a deviation). **§5:** catalog keys `ansicht.split_resize` · `ansicht.split_collapse` · `ansicht.layout_merken`. **§6:** split-pane sentinel. ToDo extended by one v0.45 item. Source: exclusively this chat + the existing v0.44.
- 2026-07-14 · v0.44 · **Search/filter mindsets (author instruction for Ledger & Lines and all tables: filter always above the table, highly sortable/filterable with boolean logic, and search-within-results as its own function — "some minds think this way" — plus a note to first improve the base search).** **New Golden Rules (§2):** `[STANDARD]` **the filter bar always sits above the table and governs everything beneath it** (active state + "X of Y" counter visible; no unshown condition may take effect); `[STANDARD]` **boolean filter logic in full** (AND/OR/NOT + bracket groups, combinable with full-text, savable as a named view); `[STANDARD]` **search-within-results as a visible staged chain** (`suchen.in_ergebnissen`: every refinement strictly ⊆ the previous stage, chain display "1,304,328 → 12,944 → 231," stages individually removable, changing the base search resets with a warning) with a **mindset hint** on first refining ("tip: first check whether an improved base search is what's meant") and a **standard short example** in the help text (base search "status = open" → yesterday's *closed* invoice 2023 exists in the database but not in the subset — the refinement inherits all the restrictions and blind spots of the first search; mnemonic: *"search-within-results can only narrow — it can never discover what the first search excluded"*). **§5:** catalog keys `filter.boolesch` + `suchen.in_ergebnissen`; construction-pattern requirement filter-bar-on-top. **§6:** search/filter construction-pattern sentinel. All items simultaneously serve as migration cards for the ledger gap list. ToDo extended by two v0.44 items. Source: exclusively this chat + the existing v0.43.
- 2026-07-14 · v0.43 · **Table-capability switch catalog, extracted from `ledger-and-lines-v12_4_2_.html` (author instruction: take all table/list rules from the ledger and fix them as configurable functions, switchable per role).** **§5 catalog** (groups A–I, each capability a switch key): Structure (insert rows/columns · duplicate · delete · move; split cells h/v · merge; sheet/folder) · Data (sort/filter per column, full-text, multi-/range selection, **live selection statistics**) · Calculation (formula picker, recalculation — v0.9 protection applies) · Formatting (text formats, row/column colors, banding, skins, language) · **Import** (clipboard schema, file, **image/PDF/screenshot with confidence**) · Export (CSV/XLSX/PDF/HTML, at least one path round-trip-capable) · History (undo, change highlighting) · Collaboration (collaboration tier, identity/team, activity feed with filters, notifications, visible sync status) · Help. **New Golden Rules (§2):** `[STANDARD]` every capability = a named switch per scope × role with a default profile per template, hide-not-delete, **OFF enforced at the point of action** (visibility ≠ security); `[HARD]` **switches only narrow — they never open up** (effective capability ⊆ rights matrix; ledger integrity/audit/MANDATORY-/data-class gates are not switchable; no switch ever permits altering booked entries); `[STANDARD]` **confidence import is a proposal** (human acceptance required; the procedure honestly carries its I-tier; I2 extraction only through provider-/data-class-/budget governance); `[STANDARD]` honest work/sync status + selection statistics as an aggregate + change highlighting. **§6:** Capability-switch sentinel [HARD]. **Dual benefit:** the catalog is the starting inventory of the ledger gap list — the delta requirements (saved views, column filters, round-trip export, role profiles, endpoint enforcement) are the first migration cards. ToDo extended by three v0.43 items. Source: exclusively this chat + the ledger file + the existing v0.42.
- 2026-07-14 · v0.42 · **Table/list construction pattern, ledger integrity, product/app register (author questions: do table/filter rules exist? raise the ledger to master level; app overview — list in the master or in Humpl?).** Finding: task filter lists (v0.24), clipboard import (v0.12), calculation protection (v0.9) existed — a generic table construction pattern, ledger rules, and a product register were missing. **New Golden Rules (§2):** `[STANDARD]` **Table/list construction pattern** (capability catalog for every data table: column choice/order · sorting · column filter + full-text · **saved, shareable filter views** · aggregate counter row == filtered rows · **export as a clipboard round-trip** with a schema row — every export re-importable · handling of large quantities without silent truncation · rights-accurate actions · a speaking empty state); `[HARD]` **Ledger integrity** — booked entries are immutable, **correction via reversal/counter-entry with a reference to the original, never by overwriting**; balances exclusively an aggregate of individual bookings; an audit record per booking; retention/record-keeping obligations per jurisdiction via Lex (an instance of hide-not-delete + audit trail + aggregate principle, applied to bookkeeping); `[STANDARD]` **one product/app register** — **the rule in the master, the list in the tool** (Humpl/Universe project world, dogfooding v0.35; the master is a rule set, not an aging inventory — §18): per started app: status (including "paused with follow-up"), maintenance/developer role, verified **master status**, coverage-board link (v0.35), migration phase; permanently answers "what was started, what needs finishing, what is up to date." **§5:** Table-construction-pattern entry. **§6:** three sentinels (table construction pattern · **ledger integrity [HARD]** · app register). **Ledger** noted as the first migration candidate of the old→new path (gap list against the new rules). ToDo extended by four v0.42 items. Source: exclusively this chat + the existing v0.41.
- 2026-07-14 · v0.41 · **§21 Universe Mail — the rule-mirroring application (author instruction: the Universe offers its own mail software, online and offline, that honors all Universe rules).** Core sentence: **"Email is a channel of the control-loop system, not a second filing system."** **New Golden Rules (§2):** `[HARD]` **Mail is a channel, not storage** (business matters live as control-loop instances, never only in the inbox; case binding via `[REF]` + sub-addressing; unbound business mail → an assignment control loop with a deadline); `[STANDARD]` **all rules apply** (outgoing mail passes through the v0.40 communication review lane including recipient language, draft origin, and a **mandatory signature** as a MANDATORY dimension — content per jurisdiction via Lex; incoming status never unverified, v0.7; AI/ML only through the switch matrix, the **data-class gate**, and budget — mail content never reaches external AI unfiltered; encryption + tenant keys); `[STANDARD]` **offline mail with an honest queue** (reading/searching/composing fully offline; a visible outbound queue with status per mail and a receipt control loop — no mail silently lost; a MANDATORY-I2 dimension offline → witnessing pending or send waits; **the internet disconnection test applies to Universe Mail just as it does to SCLAN**); `[STANDARD]` **retention as a §12 control loop** (business mail = a data object with class/steward/rule; deadlines per jurisdiction via Lex, nothing invented). **§21 contains:** Incoming (verification, case binding, I1 triage, §19 linkage) · Outgoing (command language, templates, review lane, freshness anchor on attachments) · Offline · switched AI/ML with visible AI status in the composer · retention & security · management view + migration of existing mailboxes via the old→new path (v0.35). **§6:** Universe-Mail sentinel [HARD]. **§1:** collective prompt extended to §1–§21. Sentinels: **instruction-seam sentinel [HARD]**, **block-justification sentinel [HARD]**, **cross-border-crossing acknowledgment sentinel**. **§7 avatars: for the first time seven named avatars** entered (Prof, Lingua, Coach, Aegis, Aria, Vera, Lex) — previously `[OPEN]`. *(Category confirmed by the author: avatars, not sentinels; the avatar→sentinel mapping open, see ToDo.)* §8: generic data pattern "order object at the seam"; concrete legal bases (§ 2a SchwarzArbG [Undeclared Work Act], § 17 MiLoG [Minimum Wage Act], § 28a SGB IV, § 7a/§ 7 para. 4 SGB VI, AÜG [Temporary Employment Act], EU Directive 2024/2831) deliberately kept **not** global but project-specific. Do-NOTs: ten new entries. Duplicates/conflicts: seven new notices, including a **category clarification, avatar (§7) ≠ sentinel (§6)** — mapping table open. **ToDo list newly introduced at the end of the file**; the collective prompt (§1) extended to cover continuation + avatar/sentinel separation. **No project-specific one-off adopted** (Marès/Francesco/figures remain in the project knowledge base).
- 2026-07-12 · v0.5 · Collected from the **Staufenberg / compliance-gate chat** (generic): the starting point was the question of why existing check instances (sentinels) hadn't warned about the missing imprint/privacy policy — the insight: the built **AccessGate** checks *access per click*, what was missing was a gate that checks *system state before go-live*. Golden Rules: **go-live gate/compliance gate [HARD]** (a mode-switch gate, not disableable via configuration); **active enforcement instead of a passive placeholder / "display ≠ gate" [HARD]**; **human attestation as a field** (checked-by/checked-on). Standards: **mandatory-disclosure register** (four fields per requirement); **graded compliance severity** (blocker/warning/notice); **placeholder convention** (reserved `.test` TLD + visibly styled placeholders instead of fake values). Rights: write permission on legal mandatory fields only for an authorized role, never sales/end user. Sentinels: **compliance-gate/go-live sentinel [HARD]** and **placeholder sentinel**; the generic sentinel pattern extended with the **state-gate-on-mode-switch** type. Do-NOTs: four new entries. §8: Staufenberg project reference (only the pattern adopted; concrete legal bases — § 5 DDG [Digital Services Act], HRB/registry court/VAT-ID, §§ 34c/34f GewO [Trade Regulation Act], Capital Investment Act — deliberately kept **not** global but project-specific). Duplicates/conflicts: four new notices. **Avatars still `[OPEN]`** (raised in the chat, but none defined).
- 2026-07-12 · v0.4 · Collected from the **[mn]medianet / SCLAN 5.0 chat** (generic): Golden Rules (development time ≠ runtime / no compiler in the field [HARD]; three-column make-or-buy; injection protection, all five vectors [HARD]; cryptographic provability [HARD]; risk-proportional key rotation; grace-period overlap; forensics via audit trail; clipboard as a universal importer via a schema line; a universal command language everywhere; a persistent command bar at the bottom of the page; a strict parser before the AI layer; timezone upkeep across three contexts; local time behind a person's name; financial/legal-advice disclaimer [HARD]; a rate inquiry ≠ a credit application). Roles: a generic tier model T1–T7 with rotation windows and hard caps added. Rights matrix: T1–T7 rows + a key-rotation column. Standards: ReportLab as a PDF alternative; interactive HTML deliverables with sliders; schema-tag import; cryptography standard; KMS hierarchy; compliance mapping; slider precision (2 decimal places, de-DE); document house style (Navy/Amber/Helvetica). Do-NOTs: six new entries. Sentinels: injection sentinel, key-rotation-age sentinel, disclaimer sentinel, schema-import sentinel, timezone-completeness sentinel. Duplicates/conflicts: two new notices.
